NOTIFICATIONS BY GOVERNMENT
LAW DEPARTMENT
LIST OF NOTARIES APPOINTED BY GOVERNMENT OF TAMIL NADU AND IN PRACTICE AT THE BEGINNING
OF THE YEAR 2026
[G.O.Ms.No.15, Law (Administration), 30th January 2026,
No. II(2)/LAW/28(e)/2026.
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the Notaries Act, 1952 (Central Act 53 of 1952), Governor of
Tamil Nadu hereby publishes the following list of Notaries appointed by the Government of Tamil Nadu and in practice at
the beginning of the year 2026.
